Generated by python src/digitalmodel/solvers/orcaflex/model_catalogue.py --output docs/domains/orcaflex/library/model_catalogue.html. Inputs: library/model_library, templates, library/templates and claim registry/inventory under docs/domains/orcaflex; path references in tests, examples/workflows and docs/registry/workflows.yaml. Staleness is detected by test_committed_page_is_fresh in tests/solvers/orcaflex/test_model_catalogue.py, comparing committed bytes with two consecutive builds.

Plain-reader failures in the repository's master-file form: 54; all master.yml files are counted; includes are not assembled.

Counting rule: object lists count entries; named object mappings count definitions (New/Deleted are directives). Include-only objects count under IncludeFile; BaseFile/IncludeFile references are counted, never expanded. Specification objects are labelled generic.section; other top-level sections are not object counts.

One card per YAML source variant; includes are not assembled. No loadability or solved-shape claim is implied. Selected environment fields only; values retain file units. Missing fields are not stated.
